    With dinner gone, they don't really notice how Kara has settled against the bean bags and pillows, how Lena managed to sit between her legs and lean against Kara's front. Kara's arms are around her shoulders, Lena's head is nestled on the blonde's chest, and she feels Kara's laughter and every breath against her bones.

    They cuddle under the growing darkness and the stars, and in the warmth of Kara's arms, while watching animated fish look for Nemo, Lena realizes that it has been so long since that godforsaken day she had acquired Kara's services.

    "Can I ask you something?" Lena says when the credits for the movie roll.

    Kara chuckles. "So this is how it feels from the other side."

    Lena giggles, angles her head up to meet Kara's eyes in the dimness of the area. Kara shifts but doesn't let go of Lena; her arms are still wrapped around her shoulders, and Lena squeezes the blonde's arm resting against her collarbones. "You said you knew, even back when you knew me as Tess." Kara chuckles at that. Lena smiles and continues. "How?"

    Kara takes a moment. "I didn't know," she admits with a smile. "I mean, I didn't realize until sometime later. But it's—I don't know how to describe it? It was only later, in hindsight. You made me… Feel. Things." She scrunches her nose. "Okay, that sounds weirder said out loud."

    Lena swats her arm playfully and laughs. "I get what you mean, you dork. I remember feeling like I could easily let you in," Lena admits. "Even when I knew nothing about you. Which was… Confusing for  me. I had what, one friend?" She laughs, remembers how Kara had reminded her of the sun, how she couldn't help but surrender herself to Kara's warmth. It was unusual for her to feel like that, like she could entrust her whole world to a mere stranger and feel nothing but warmth.

    And yet.

    "And here we are now," Kara murmurs. She pauses, then Lena feels lips against her temple. She sighs fondly.

    "Here we are."

    They bask in each other's warmth, with nothing but the company of each other and the distant sounds of the city. Lena mindlessly draws shapes against Kara's soft skin as they lay in silence that Kara breaks some time later.

    "We should go in, it's getting colder," she suggests.

    Lena nods, but neither of them move. There is a question that Lena hesitates to ask, but after a while manages to with an almost whisper. "Do you want me to stay the night?"

    She feels Kara suck in a breath against her. Kara ducks her head, lips practically brushing against Lena's hair. "Is it okay for you to stay over? It's our first date and all."

    Lena shrugs. "Why not?" She asks, navigating blindly through the unfamiliar terrain that practically felt like home with Kara. She finally pulls away to face to the blonde, settling beside her this time. "Stop thinking like a date and be my best friend for a moment."

    At that, Kara smiles. She reaches for the collar of Lena's denim jacket and tugs on it lightly. "I'll always be your best friend," Kara promises.

    Lena chuckles; she places her hand on Kara's on her collar, taking the blonde's attention. "What if I ask you to be my girlfriend?" Lena teases.

    Kara pauses, then actually bursts into giggles. "I'd say yes, but then you'd be my girlfriend and best friend."

    "Your girlfriend, huh?" Lena beams. The word echoes in her head, makes her heartbeat skip, and in hindsight, maybe she should have asked more seriously, but Kara's expression turns a little serious, too, and she bites her lip.

    "Only if you were serious," Kara replies with a blush.

    Lena hesitates. "I- I mean, I was teasing," she admits, "but I- Is that even a thing people do on first dates?"

    Kara tugs on her collar again. "I don't have the best frames of reference for that one," she admits with a little smile. "I dated like, twice in the past."

    Lena pokes her hand. "Not helping."

    Kara sighs. "I don't know," she whispers. She meets Lena's gaze; there is softness in her blue eyes that doesn't help in calming Lena's heart, and her mind is screaming for her to actually ask. "Ask me something else," Kara suggests with a whisper.

    Lena makes a small sound. "You're the journalist."

    Kara licks her lips. "Okay." She looks at Lena's collar, on the hand on hers. She lets go of the fabric and turns her hand, takes Lena's instead and threads their fingers together before pulling Lena's hand against her chest. Lena swallows at the pounding heartbeat she feels against the back of her own hand, the rhythm similar to the one in her own chest. Kara looks in her eyes.

    "Does it feel right?" Kara finally asks.

    Lena cups Kara's cheeks, drowns in the ocean of her eyes all over again, and it's like looking at a sunrise, how all of her feels like a beginning. Kara is warm and it burns in the best of ways—and she thinks she can go through all the doubt and the preemptive heartache she has gone through before over and over, if it means having this moment here. She bites her lip, searches for the courage Kara had found days before, at the heart of a penguin exhibit, anchors herself in all of Kara in this moment, here.

    "Yes."

    epilogue;

    Chapter Notes

    day one

    The first thing Lena does upon waking up is wonder if she is still dreaming.

    She wakes up with softness against her cheek, skin tickled with blonde hair and Kara’s steady breathing against her ear. Kara’s arms are wrapped around her, like Kara, even in her sleep, is trying to defend her from whatever there lies in dreams, and Lena finds herself smiling.

    It feels like a dream, if she was being honest, but it isn’t. She stirs slightly in Kara’s arms as she feels the warmth of the sun filtering through the curtains in Kara’s bedroom. She wonders what time it is. They had gotten in last night after watching Her—falling in love is kind of like a form of socially acceptable insanity, Amy said, and Lena could only look at Kara then—then watched Finding Nemo, as promised, in the living room, with the entirely-too-soft penguin plushie Lena got for Kara in the CEO’s arms.

    “It’s a sight to see,” Kara said, and Lena just laughed and said no one would believe her if she told people. Kara only shrugged. “I don’t think anyone would believe me if I tell them you’re my girlfriend either, so…”

    Lena was speechless for a moment, felt her cheeks heat up just as Kara apologized, and Lena assured her with a soft kiss on her lips that it was definitely more than fine.

    Her girlfriend. Imagine that.

    Kara grumbles sleepily and seems to hold Lena tighter, making the raven-haired woman smile and squeeze her back. A few moments later, Kara pulls back slightly, eyes half-open as she smiles at Lena.

    It should be unfair, how easy it was for her to pull a smile from Lena, too.

    “Good morning,” Kara greets, voice still husky with sleep. She still has her arms around Lena though, not really pulling away completely, just enough for her to look the woman in the eyes. “Have you been awake long?”

    Lena shakes her head. “Just woke up.” She shifts, legs sliding against Kara’s just as the blonde pulls Lena into her arms again, as if satisfied with Lena’s answer. Lena sighs, warmth and contentment in her bones as she buries her face against Kara’s neck again. She feels more than hears Kara hum.

    “You okay?” Kara asks.

    Lena nods. “More than,” she whispers, eyes closed. Her lips brush against Kara’s skin and she feels Kara stiffen for a moment. “You know. You really are good with cuddles.”

    Kara giggles. “I did it professionally, once.”

    Lena laughs, lightly nudging the side of Kara’s head. “Yeah?” A hum, like Lena is considering the question. “Why’d you quit then?”

    “Got promoted,” Kara replies quickly, then hums as if in thought. “And… I met this woman.”

    That captures Lena’s attention. She pulls back slightly, a lazy, teasing smile on her lips as she arches her eyebrow, her still-sleepy gaze locked on Kara’s. Without her glasses, Lena is given a close-up view of eyes as blue as the ocean and as warm as the sun.

    “Oh?” Lena manages, breathless. “Do tell.”

    “Stop that,” Kara laughs, moving to press her forehead against Lena’s as she loosens her hold, just for a moment, only to sigh and pull Lena ever closer. “Well… I realized I didn’t really want to hold anyone else like that but this certain woman,” she says softly, almost as if she’s embarrassed to admit it. Lena thinks she is blushing, but they are too close to tell. “You know. Professionally or otherwise.”

    Then Kara whines, before burying her face in Lena’s hair. “It sounded less cheesier in my head.”

    Lena is caught off-guard but she laughs and runs her fingers through Kara’s hair, even as she feels her own heart race and her cheeks heat up. “That… does sound cheesy,” Lena murmurs with a soft laugh.

    Kara sighs. Not in the way when one’s tired, or frustrated, but like she feels this is a dream as much as Lena thinks it is. “Doesn’t make it any less true,” Kara says.

    Lena melts in her arms fully now, nothing holding her whole but Kara herself. “Sounds like she’s one lucky woman.”

    “I think we both are.” Kara smiles and kisses Lena’s temple before pulling her into her arms again, squeezing her lightly and making the dark-haired woman giggle. Laughter echoes in the cozy silence of Kara’s room, at least until they both hear the unlocking of the front door. Kara pauses and tilts her head up, as if waiting to see who would appear by the open doorway to her bedroom.

    She gets her answer after two heartbeats of too-quick pitter-patter of feet, then there is a weight on Lena’s side. Krypto barks then licks Lena’s face.

    “I’m right here!” Kara tells Krypto, sounding completely offended, but she does sit up. Lena laughs as she lies on her back, trying to hold back the overeager puppy from slobbering over her face.

    “I missed you too, buddy,” Lena coos, rubbing his belly and letting him squirm away contently on Lena’s lap.

    “Hey you two,” a voice says, and Lena looks up to find Alex by the doorway, dressed up in her usual shirt and leather jacket. She grins as Lena and Kara give her a wave hello. “Sorry for dropping Krypto back. I got a briefing in half an hour and Maggie’s still at a scene.”

    Kara looks confused. She turns to her bedside table to take her glasses and puts them on with a little crinkle of her eyebrows that makes Lena smile.

    “What time is it?” Kara asks.

    Alex frowns. “It’s almost ten?” She shrugs and gestures to the door. “Anyway, I have to go. Later you two!”

    She leaves before the pair on the bed could even say anything; Krypto just barks after the older Danvers before returning his attention to Lena and Kara, panting as he looks up at the blonde.

    “I will not forget this betrayal,” Kara tells the puppy. Krypto barks once, and Lena watches Kara swallow thickly as if she is fighting back the urge to hold him.

    With a playful roll of her eyes, Lena pushes Krypto to Kara’s arms. Krypto responds with enthusiastic licks on Kara’s face. The blonde breaks after a full second and bursts into laughing and Lena—

    Lena doesn’t quite think she has ever been this happy.

    “Hey,” Kara whispers, pulling Lena away from her little daydream. Lena turns to her with what she hopes isn’t a lovesick smile. Kara scratches Krypto behind his ears, the puppy softly woofing with satisfaction, and the way she smiles looks like the way Lena feels: weightless. “Can I tell you something?”

    Lena chuckles. “Kara…”

    “I love you.” Kara’s eyes widen as soon as the words spill out and Lena thinks her expression is the same: lips parted open in surprise, cheeks flushed, heart racing. Kara visibly swallows and focuses her attention on Krypto, petting him as if it was a nervous tick and the puppy knew no better than to enjoy the attention. Kara bites her lip. “I'm- I’m in love with you. I was going to wait, but I feel like there’s no point to because, well,” she laughs awkwardly, looks down at Krypto, then back at Lena, “it’s really a long time coming, and you’re here and I feel like that and—”

    “Hey,” Lena interrupts softly. She smiles and reaches out for Kara to cup her cheek, trying to ignore the fact that her hand is trembling. Kara seems to relax at her touch and she smiles back, and it’s all Lena needs; her sun and sea, looking at her like she created all Kara knows. “I love you, too.”

    Kara melts, sinks into the warmth of Lena’s hand with a sigh that is relieved, like she could finally breathe. “I- I want to kiss you,” she says.

    Lena grins, wide and happy, as she moves closer to cup Kara’s face with both hands before leaning in to capture Kara’s lips in a kiss, soft like their first.

    Krypto barks between them.

    The two women laugh and pull apart. Lena pats Krypto’s head before Kara brings him up so she could kiss the top of his head. “Yeah, I love you too, buddy,” Kara laughs.

    As if on cue, Kara’s stomach grumbles. They laugh again, but Lena rolls her eyes playfully before rolling out of bed. Kara whines, but Lena shakes her head and takes her phone. “I’m not going to let you starve on my watch, Kara.”

    “Yes but!” Kara gestures to the bed. “It’s  more comfortable here, and we can just order pizza or something and cuddle while we’re waiting.”

    Lena raises an eyebrow.

    “Come on,” Kara laughs. “It’s a good plan.”

    Lena narrows her eyes, trying to look for an excuse, but with Kara beaming at her like that with an offer for warmth and snuggles, it was difficult to say no. She doesn’t say anything, just shakes her head before dialing for Kara’s favorite pizza place and placing the order, and Kara bumps a fist in the air before lying back down. Krypto jumps out of her grasp, just as Lena ends the call. Kara laughs, but he moves down to make himself comfortable by the blonde’s legs.

    “You think he’s pissed at me for stealing you?” Lena asks with a small, amused glance at the puppy.

    “Are you kidding?” Kara says, opening her arms for Lena to slip into. “He probably loves you more than he loves me. Now get in here.”

    Lena remembers the first time—the unfamiliarity of human contact and cuddling, this stranger with sunshine on her smile. Those same words. Nodding, she slips under the sheets as well, and with the ease of coming home, settles beside Kara. Strong, warm arms wrap around Lena’s body and she’s being pulled flush into Kara’s front. She finds her face resting against the crook of Kara’s neck, Kara’s cheek pressed against her temple. A small sound of contentment makes itself known and she feels the woman against her chuckle.

    She thinks she imagines it, the way Kara’s heart races like hers does.

    “I love you,” Kara says again, minutes later when Lena thinks she has fallen asleep, “and I hope you know I’m definitely not letting you go.”

    month four

    Three sharp knocks echo in Lena’s apartment, and then silence. Kara freezes like a deer caught in headlights as Lena laughs and stands.

    “It’s gonna be fine, Kara,” she assures, before heading to the door to open it. Kara stands, too, nervously wiping her sweaty palms on her jeans before following Lena to the doorway. Lena looks at her for a moment before opening the door.

    “Hello, mother,” she greets, and moves to pull Lillian into a hug. The older Luthor sighs and accepts it with a smile, eyes closing for a moment before fixing a glare on the blonde frozen still behind Lena.

    “Kara Danvers,” Lillian greets, voice detached, her face a neutral mask.

    Kara glances at Lena before grinning at the older Luthor, though it seemed a bit forced. Lena pulls away from the hug to look at the two of them.

    “Ma’am.” Kara clears her throat, then offers her hand for Lillian to shake. “It’s very nice to finally, officially meet you.”

    “What are you plans with my daughter?” Lillian asks, right off the bat.

    Lena rolls her eyes and sighs tiredly. “Really, mother?”

    Lillian looks at Lena and shrugs. “Am I not supposed to scare her or something since your father isn’t around to do it?”

    “She’s really nervous about meeting you,” Lena says, taking Kara’s trembling hand.

    “I can see that,” Lillian says, unamused.

    Kara stammers. “I…”

    Lena shakes her head. “One threat, and we’re getting dinner. Please. You’ll make Kara cry.”

    Kara huffs at Lena. “I’m not going to—”

    “Ms. Danvers,” Lillian begins.

    Kara stands ramrod straight and turns to her. “Kara, Ma’am."

    “Ms. Danvers,” Lillian repeats flatly. “I know you have good intentions. And I’ve never quite seen Lena this happy, and I think much of that has to do with you, but know this. I can make your life a living hell.”

    Lillian smiles sweetly and Lena sighs, but even she can’t hold back her smile as Kara gapes like a fish out of water.

    “Ma’am,” Kara says after a few seconds. “I promise—”

    “Lillian,” the older Luthor says. She finally cracks a smile. “Maybe Mother, sometime soon, if you’re lucky.”

    “Mom,” Lena interrupts, glaring at the woman, though her cheeks dust pink.

    “Now let’s go,” Lillian says, walking past the blushing couple and into Lena's apartment like she had many times before. “I’m famished.”  

    year 3

    “That’s cheating!” Lena yells.

    Kara laughs as she lets the waves wash over her feet. Krypto barks as he runs along with her, used to the water now. The sun sinks in the horizon, coloring everything with fire and warmth, but it’s nothing compared to the way Lena feels as she watches Kara laugh, so at home in the beach like Lena was with her.

    “It’s not my fault you easily get distracted by my arms,” Kara shoots back, and as if to make a point, she flexes again, the sleeves of her Hawaiian shirt tight against her biceps.

    “Still cheating,” Lena laughs. She finally makes her way to Kara. She feels the cool water on her bare feet and her pounding heart in her chest.

    “I still won,” Kara giggles, then moves in to press a kiss on Lena’s temple. Lena smiles. She doesn’t remember Maldives being this beautiful, or herself being this happy. The setting sun feels like a new beginning.

    “Hey,” Lena says, pulling Kara’s attention away from the sunset. “Can I ask you something?”

    Kara grins. Lena aches in the best of ways, home in her blue eyes. “Shoot.”

    Lena bites her lip and fishes the ring from her shirt pocket. “Will you marry me?”

    Kara pulls her in her arms, holds her like she never dared let go, and whispers a promise of a lifetime in Lena’s ear. “A thousand times yes, Lena.”

    decade one

    “Laura, honey, please don’t give Streaky too many treats, okay?” Lena calls out from the living room. She barely hears her four-year-old daughter’s distracted response, but Kara’s voice filters in from the kitchen soon after.

    “I got it, babe, don’t worry!” A crash. “Krypto, no!”

    Lena sighs and turns from her seat to the kitchen. “Kara, honey, you need help with that?”

    “I got it! Sit your pretty butt down!”

    Lena giggles and turns to her visitor.

    Veronica rolls her eyes from where she sits across Lena. “God, you two are still fucking disgusting after all these years.”

    Lena laughs and shrugs, happiness in her eyes and laughter lines on her face. “Weren’t you the one who told me to give it a shot?”

     

     

    the end

    and you, my love, are the sea
